	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API -->
    <script type="text/javascript">

			function printOut(str) {
			    document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
			    document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
			}

			function runMacro() {

			    //This catches any errors and exits out of the code if caught
			    //Errors are displayed in Output field of form
			    try {
			   		apiCode();
			    }
			        catch (err) {
			            printOut(djErrorString(err));
			        }
				}

			function apiCode() {
			    var mScript = new djScriptManager();
			    var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
			    mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
			    var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
			    var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
			    var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
			    var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X").value);
			    var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y").value);
			    var Z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z").value);
			    var R = parseFloat(document.getElementById("R").value)
			    var mCircle = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X, Y, Z, R);

			    //Play around with the custom data
			    var appName = document.getElementById("A").value;

			    //Begin the custom data coding
			    var custom1 = mCircle.GetCustomData(appName);

			    var index = 0;
			    var marker = 1000;

			    printOut("Custom data...");
			    printOut(" ");

			    //Testing string, marker, and type
			    custom1.InsertStringData(index, marker, "Circle entity");
			    var testString = custom1.GetStringData(index);
			    printOut("  Original string: " + JSON.stringify(testString));
			    custom1.SetStringData(index, "Circle entity changed");
			    testString = custom1.GetStringData(index);
			    printOut("  Modified string: " + JSON.stringify(testString));
			    var testMarker = custom1.GetDataMarker(index);
			    printOut("  Marker: " + JSON.stringify(testMarker));
			    var testType = custom1.GetDataType(index);
			    printOut("  Data type: " + JSON.stringify(testType));
			    printOut(" ");

			    //Testing double
			    index = 1;
			    marker = 1021;
			    custom1.InsertDoubleData(index, marker, 7.0);
			    var testDouble = custom1.GetDoubleData(index);
			    printOut("  Original double: " + JSON.stringify(testDouble));
			    custom1.SetDoubleData(index, 12.0);
			    testDouble = custom1.GetDoubleData(index);
			    printOut("  Modified double: " + JSON.stringify(testDouble));
			    var testMarker = custom1.GetDataMarker(index);
			    printOut("  Marker: " + JSON.stringify(testMarker));
			    testType = custom1.GetDataType(index);
			    printOut("  Data type: " + JSON.stringify(testType));
   			    printOut(" ");

			    //Testing Int16 and Int32
			    index = 2;
			    marker = 1070;
			    custom1.InsertInteger16Data(index, marker, 10);
			    var testInt16 = custom1.GetInteger16Data(index);
			    printOut("  Original Int16: " + JSON.stringify(testInt16));
			    custom1.SetInteger16Data(index, 20);
			    testInt16 = custom1.GetInteger16Data(index);
			    printOut("  Modified Int16: " + JSON.stringify(testInt16));
			     var testMarker = custom1.GetDataMarker(index);
			    printOut("  Marker: " + JSON.stringify(testMarker));
			    testType = custom1.GetDataType(index);
			    printOut("  Data type: " + JSON.stringify(testType));
			    printOut(" ");

			    index = 3;
			    marker = 1071;
			    custom1.InsertInteger32Data(index, marker, 30);
			    var testInt32 = custom1.GetInteger32Data(index);
			    printOut("  Original Int32: " + JSON.stringify(testInt32));
			    custom1.SetInteger32Data(index, 40);
			    testInt32 = custom1.GetInteger32Data(index);
   			    printOut("  Modified Int32: " + JSON.stringify(testInt32));
			    var testMarker = custom1.GetDataMarker(index);
			    printOut("  Marker: " + JSON.stringify(testMarker));
			    testType = custom1.GetDataType(index);
			    printOut("  Data type: " + JSON.stringify(testType));
			    printOut(" ");

			    //Testing the Layer name
			    index = 4;
			    marker = 1003;
			    custom1.InsertLayerName(index, "Original Layer name");
			    var testLayer = custom1.GetLayerName(index);
			    printOut("  Original Layer name: " + JSON.stringify(testLayer));
			    custom1.SetLayerName(index, "Modified Layer name");
			    testLayer = custom1.GetLayerName(index);
			    printOut("  Modified Layer name: " + JSON.stringify(testLayer));
			    var testMarker = custom1.GetDataMarker(index);
			    printOut("  Marker: " + JSON.stringify(testMarker));
			    testType = custom1.GetDataType(index);
			    printOut("  Data type: " + JSON.stringify(testType));
			    printOut(" ");

			    //Testing the byte array
			    index = 5;
			    marker = 1004;
			    var bArray = new Array();
			    bArray[0] = 3;
			    bArray[1] = 4;
			    bArray[2] = 5;
			    bArray[3] = 6;
			    custom1.InsertByteData(index, bArray);
			    var testArray = custom1.GetByteData(index);
			    for(var i = 0; i<testArray.length; i++){
			    	var arrElement = testArray[i];
			    	printOut("  Original binary data element " + i + ": " + JSON.stringify(arrElement));
			    }
			    var cArray = new Array();
			    cArray[0] = 9;
			    cArray[1] = 10;
			    cArray[2] = 11;
			    cArray[3] = 12;
			    custom1.SetByteData(index, cArray);
			    testArray = custom1.GetByteData(index);
			    for(var i = 0; i<testArray.length; i++){
			    	var arrElement = testArray[i];
			    	printOut("  Modified binary data element " + i + ": " + JSON.stringify(arrElement));
			    }
			    var testMarker = custom1.GetDataMarker(index);
			    printOut("  Marker: " + JSON.stringify(testMarker));
			    testType = custom1.GetDataType(index);
			    printOut("  Data type: " + JSON.stringify(testType));
			    printOut(" ");

			    //Print number of custom data;
			    index =  custom1.GetDataCount();
			    printOut("  Number of custom data added to Circle: " + String(index));

            	var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0]
            	var upperRightArray = [0,0,0]
            	mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray)

			    mApp.releaseAll();
			}

</script>

<<< Attach Block Definition Annotation to Leader Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Attach_Block_Definition_Annotation_to_Leader_Example_JS.md

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsBlockDefinition.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLeader.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathPoint.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathUtility.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLine.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();

            //Abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

            //Get document, model, and Sketch Manager
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();

            //Construct a line
            var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(1, 1, 0, 6, 1, 0);

            //Constuct another line
            var mLine2 = mSkMgr.InsertLine(2, 2, 0, 2, 20, 0);

            var longArray = new Array();
            longArray[0] = 21;
            longArray[1] = 21;

            var entityArray = new Array();
            entityArray[0] = mLine;
            entityArray[1] = mLine2;

            var mBlockDefinition = mDoc.CreateBlockDefinition("MyBlockAnnotation", "Block annotation to attach to Leader", 0, 0, 0, longArray, entityArray, "dsBlockDefinitionEntities_ConvertToBlock");
            printOut(mBlockDefinition.GetName());

            //Add Leader
            var leaderCoordinates = new Array();
            leaderCoordinates[0] = 6.5;
            leaderCoordinates[1] = 7.2;
            leaderCoordinates[2] = 0;
            leaderCoordinates[3] = 7.7;
            leaderCoordinates[4] = 8.6;
            leaderCoordinates[5] = 0;
            var noteWidth = 1.0;
            var noteText = "L1 ";
            var mLeader = mSkMgr.InsertLeader(leaderCoordinates, noteWidth, noteText);

            //Create point where to insert Block definition annotation
            var firstX = 10;
            var firstY = 20;
            var firstZ = 0;
            var mMathUtility = mApp.GetMathUtility();
            var mMathPoint = mMathUtility.CreatePoint(firstX, firstY, firstZ);

           //Attach Tolerance annotation
           mLeader.SetBlockAnnotation("MyBlockAnnotation", mMathPoint);

           //Zoom to fit
           var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0]
           var upperRightArray = [0,0,0]
           mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray)

           //Type your JavaScript API code here
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <!-- Change the title so that it describes what the example demonstrates -->

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCommandMessage.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLeader.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathPoint.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathPlane.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();

            //Abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

            //Get document, model, and Sketch Manager
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();

            //Add first Leader
            var leaderCoordinates = new Array();
            leaderCoordinates[0] = 42.5;
            leaderCoordinates[1] = 20;
            leaderCoordinates[2] = 0;
            leaderCoordinates[3] = 75;
            leaderCoordinates[4] = 65;
            leaderCoordinates[5] = 0;
            var noteWidth = 1.0;
            var noteText = "L1 ";
            var mLeader = mSkMgr.InsertLeader(leaderCoordinates, noteWidth, noteText);

            //Create point where to insert Tolerance annotation
            var firstX = 20;
            var firstY = 20;
            var firstZ = 20;
            var mMathUtility = mApp.GetMathUtility();
            var mMathPoint = mMathUtility.CreatePoint(firstX, firstY, firstZ);

            //Attach Tolerance annotation
            mLeader.SetToleranceAnnotation("{\\Fgdt;j}%%v{\\Fgdt;n}0.7{\\Fgdt;m}", mMathPoint);

            //Add second Leader
            var leaderCoordinates2 = new Array();
            leaderCoordinates2[0] = 27.5;
            leaderCoordinates2[1] = 10;
            leaderCoordinates2[2] = 0;
            leaderCoordinates2[3] = 9;
            leaderCoordinates2[4] = 10;
            leaderCoordinates2[5] = 0;
            var noteWidth2 = 1.0;
            var noteText2 = "L2 ";
            var mLeader2 = mSkMgr.InsertLeader(leaderCoordinates2, noteWidth2, noteText2);

            //Create point where to insert Tolerance annotation
            var secondX = 30;
            var secondY = 10;
            var secondZ = 30;
            var mMathUtility2 = mApp.GetMathUtility();
            var mMathPoint2 = mMathUtility2.CreatePoint(secondX, secondY, secondZ);

            //Attach Tolerance annotation
            mLeader2.SetNoteAnnotation("New Note", mMathPoint2);

            //Zoom to fit
            var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0]
            var upperRightArray = [0,0,0]
            mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray)

            //Type your JavaScript API code here
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

	<!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->

	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplicationOptions.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsColor.js"></script>

	<!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API -->
	<script type="text/javascript">
		function printOut(str) {
			document.getElementById("OB").value += str;
			document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
		}

		function runMacro() {
			//This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
			//It displays an error in the output box
			try {
				apiCode();
			}
			catch (err) {
				printOut(djErrorString(err));
			}
		}
		function apiCode() {
			var mScript = new djScriptManager();
			var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
			mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

			//Enter in your JavaScript API here
			var mAppOptions = mApp.GetApplicationOptions();

			var ncolor1 = document.getElementById("C1").value;
			var ncolor2 = document.getElementById("C2").value;
			var ncolor3 = document.getElementById("C3").value;

			var color1 = mAppOptions.GetElementColor("dsElementColor_CommandWindowText");
			var color2 = mAppOptions.GetElementColor("dsElementColor_ModelCrosshair");
			var color3 = mAppOptions.GetElementColor("dsElementColor_ModelBackground");

			printOut("New color of: \n");
            printOut("   Text of command window: " + JSON.stringify(ncolor1) + "\n");
			printOut("   Crosshair: " + JSON.stringify(ncolor2) + "\n");
			printOut("   Background of model: " + JSON.stringify(ncolor3) + "\n");

			color1.SetNamedColor(ncolor1);
			color2.SetNamedColor(ncolor2);
			color3.SetNamedColor(ncolor3);

			mAppOptions.SetElementColor("dsElementColor_CommandWindowText", color1);
			mAppOptions.SetElementColor("dsElementColor_ModelCrosshair", color2);
			mAppOptions.SetElementColor("dsElementColor_ModelBackground", color3);

			mApp.releaseAll();
		}
	</script>

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,documents">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,command window">

    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCommandMessage.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsColor.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLayerManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLayer.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str;
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
            var mLM = mDoc.GetLayerManager();
            //Enter in your JavaScript API here

            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
                var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T1").value);
                var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T2").value);
                var Z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T3").value);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var wiiPoint = mCom.PromptForPoint("Select where you want to place the center point of the Circles.");
                var X = wiiPoint.X;
                var Y = wiiPoint.Y;
                var Z = wiiPoint.Z;
            }
            else {
                return false;
            }

            var circle1 = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X,Y,Z,2);
            var circle2 = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X,Y,Z,4);
            var circle3 = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X,Y,Z,6);
            var circle4 = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X,Y,Z,8);
            var ncolor1 = document.getElementById("C1").value;
            var ncolor2 = document.getElementById("C2").value;
            var ncolor3 = document.getElementById("C3").value;
            var ncolor4 = document.getElementById("C4").value;

            var color1 = circle1.get_Color();
            var color2 = circle2.get_Color();
            var color3 = circle3.get_Color();
            var color4 = circle4.get_Color();

            color1.SetNamedColor(ncolor1);
            color2.SetNamedColor(ncolor2);
            color3.SetNamedColor(ncolor3);
            color4.SetNamedColor(ncolor4);

            circle1.put_Color(color1);
            circle2.put_Color(color2);
            circle3.put_Color(color3);
            circle4.put_Color(color4);

            var layer1 = mLM.CreateLayer("Center");
            var layer2 = mLM.CreateLayer("SmallerMiddle");
            var layer3 = mLM.CreateLayer("BiggerMiddle");
            var layer4 = mLM.CreateLayer("Outer");

            var clayer1 = circle1.get_Layer();
            var clayer2 = circle2.get_Layer();
            var clayer3 = circle3.get_Layer();
            var clayer4 = circle4.get_Layer();

            circle1.put_Layer("Center");
            circle2.put_Layer("SmallerMiddle");
            circle3.put_Layer("BiggerMiddle");
            circle4.put_Layer("Outer");

            clayer1 = circle1.get_Layer();
            clayer2 = circle2.get_Layer();
            clayer3 = circle3.get_Layer();
            clayer4 = circle4.get_Layer();

            var lw1 = circle1.get_LineWeight();
            var lw2 = circle2.get_LineWeight();
            var lw3 = circle3.get_LineWeight();
            var lw4 = circle4.get_LineWeight();

            circle1.put_LineWeight("dsLnWt_025");
            circle2.put_LineWeight("dsLnWt_050");
            circle3.put_LineWeight("dsLnWt_100");
            circle4.put_LineWeight("dsLnWt_200");

            lw1 = circle1.get_LineWeight();
            lw2 = circle2.get_LineWeight();
            lw3 = circle3.get_LineWeight();
            lw4 = circle4.get_LineWeight();

            printOut("Center point of Circles:\n");
            printOut("X: " + X + " Y: " + Y + " Z: " + Z + "\n");
            printOut("Innermost Circle color: " + ncolor1 + "\n");
            printOut("Circle next to innermost Circle color: " + ncolor2 + "\n");
            printOut("Circle next to outermost Circle color: " + ncolor3 + "\n");
            printOut("Outmermost Circle color: " + ncolor4 + "\n\n");
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
<script type="text/javascript">

function printOut(str) {}

function loadArray() {

	var mScript = new djScriptManager();
	var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
	// abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
	mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
	var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
	var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
	var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
	var EN = document.getElementById("EN").value;
	var AB = parseFloat(document.getElementById("AB").value);
	var FA = parseFloat(document.getElementById("FA").value);
	var TN = parseFloat(document.getElementById("TN").value);
	var EX = parseFloat(document.getElementById("EX").value);
	var EY = parseFloat(document.getElementById("EY").value);
	var AX = parseFloat(document.getElementById("AX").value);
	var AY = parseFloat(document.getElementById("AY").value);

	var longArray = new Array();
	longArray[0] = 22;
	longArray[1] = 22;
	longArray[2] = 22;

	//Figure out a way to implement the long array and entity array
	var entityArray = new Array();
	entityArray[0] = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(30, 30, 0, 10);
	entityArray[1] = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(60, 60, 0, 10);
	entityArray[2] = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(90, 90, 0, 10);

	var orient = true;

	var mPattern = mSkMgr.PatternCircular(EN, AB, FA, TN, EX, EY, AX, AY, orient, longArray, entityArray);

    var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0]
    var upperRightArray = [0,0,0]
    mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray)
    mApp.releaseAll();

	mApp.releaseAll();
}

</script>

<h1>Create Circular Pattern Example (JavaScript)</h1>
    <body>
        <table style="text-align: left; width: 860px; height: 600px; font-family: Verdana, sans-serif;" border="0"
            cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0">

            <tbody>
                <tr>
                    <td style="height: 75px;">
                        <div class="style7">
							This example creates a circular pattern
							of Circles in a DraftSight drawing.<ol>
                            <li>Start DraftSight.
														<li>Using the following
														form, specify the
														parameters for the
														circular pattern:<ol>
															<li>Type the angle
															between the base
															points of the
															pattern entities and
															the center of the
															pattern in <b>Angle
															between pattern
															entities</b>. This
															field is only valid
															if you selected <b>
															Angle between and
															total number of elements </b>or&nbsp;<b>Fill
															angle and angle
															between elements</b> for <b>
															Pattern</b>.</li>
															<li>Type a positive
															or negative value
															for <b>Fill angle</b>. The default
															for the angle to
															fill is 360° - a
															full circle pattern.
															A value of 0 is
															not allowed. The
															pattern is drawn
															counterclockwise for
															positive angles and
															clockwise for
															negative angles.
															This field is only
															valid if you
															selected <b>Fill
															angle and angle</b>
															<b>between</b> <b>
															elements</b> or <b>Fill
															angle and total
															number of elements </b>for <b>
															Pattern</b>.</li>
															<li>Type the total
															number of pattern
															entities, including
															the source entity in
															<b>Number of pattern
															entities</b>.</li>
															<li>Type the x
															coordinate of the
															base point of the
															pattern in <b>Base
															point x</b>.</li>
															<li>Type the y
															coordinate of the
															base point of the
															pattern in <b>Base
															point y</b>.</li>
															<li>Type the x
															coordinate of the
															center point of the
															pattern in <b>Axis
															point x</b>.</li>
															<li>Type the y
															coordinate of the
															center point of the
															pattern in <b>Axis
															point y</b>.</li>
															<li>Select<b>
															Pattern entities
															about axis </b>to
															rotate the entities
															as they are
															patterned, or leave
															this check box clear
															to align the
															entities to the
															source entity.</li>
															<li>Click <b>Insert</b>.</li>
														</ol>
                            <li>Examine the drawing.</li>
                            </ol>
								<p><b>NOTES:</b></p>
								<ul>
									<li>Errors are printed to Output.</li>
									<li>To recreate this example:</li>
								</ul>

									<ol>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModelNamedView.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsNamedView.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSheetNamedView.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                document.getElementById("OB").value = "";
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mVMgr = mDoc.GetViewManager();
            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
            	mModel.Activate();
            	var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X").value);
                var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y").value);
                var Z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z").value);
                var bArray = new Array();
			    bArray[0] = X;
			    bArray[1] = Y;
			    bArray[2] = Z;
			    var X2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X2").value);
                var Y2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y2").value);
                var Z2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z2").value);
                var cArray = new Array();
			    cArray[0] = X2;
			    cArray[1] = Y2;
			    cArray[2] = Z2;
			    var name1 = document.getElementById("name1").value;
                var cat1 = document.getElementById("cat1").value;
				var savename1 = document.getElementById("savename1").value;
				var savecat1 = document.getElementById("savecat1").value;
                var resultStatus = mVMgr.CreateModelNamedView(name1, cat1, bArray, cArray);
                var nView = resultStatus.NamedView;
                var namedView1 = nView.GetNamedView();
                printOut("Result status of new model view creation: " + namedView1.GetName());
                mVMgr.ActivateModelView(namedView1.GetName());
                var modelNamedView = mVMgr.GetModelNamedView(name1);
                var namedView = modelNamedView.GetNamedView();
                var name = namedView.GetName();
                printOut("New model view: " + JSON.stringify(name));
                mVMgr.SaveCurrentViewAsModelView(savename1, savecat1);
                mVMgr.ActivateModelView(savename1);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var X1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X1").value);
                var Y1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y1").value);
                var Z1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z1").value);
                var dArray = new Array();
			    dArray[0] = X1;
			    dArray[1] = Y1;
			    dArray[2] = Z1;
			    var X3 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X3").value);
                var Y3 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y3").value);
                var Z3 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z3").value);
                var eArray = new Array();
			    eArray[0] = X3;
			    eArray[1] = Y3;
			    eArray[2] = Z3;
			    var name2 = document.getElementById("name2").value;
                var cat2 = document.getElementById("cat2").value;
				var savename2 = document.getElementById("savename2").value;
				var savecat2 = document.getElementById("savecat2").value;
            	var resultStatus2 = mVMgr.CreateSheetNamedView(name2, cat2, dArray, eArray);
            	var snamedview = resultStatus2.NamedView;
            	var snview = snamedview.GetNamedView();
                printOut("Result status of new sheet view creation: " + snview.GetName());
                mVMgr.ActivateSheetView(snview.GetName());
                var sheetNamedView = mVMgr.GetSheetNamedView(name2);
                var namedView2 = sheetNamedView.GetNamedView();
                name = namedView2.GetName();
                printOut("New sheet view: " + JSON.stringify(name));
                mVMgr.SaveCurrentViewAsSheetView(savename2, savecat2);
                mVMgr.ActivateSheetView(savename2);
            }
           mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<<< Create Rectangular Viewport Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Create_Rectangular_Viewport_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Viewports">

    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSheet.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsViewport.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathUtility.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathPoint.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {

            //Type your JavaScript API code here
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();

            //Abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

            //Get the active document
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();

			var mSheets = newArray();
			mSheets = mDoc.GetSheets();

			//Switch to Sheet space and activate Sheet2
			for (var index = 0; index < mSheets.length; index++){
				var mSheet = mSheets[index];
				//Get Sheet name
				var sheetName = mSheet.get_Name();
				if (sheetName != "Model"){
					//Activate Sheet
						mSheet.Activate();
				}
			}

			//Set the corners for the Viewport
			var mMathUtility = mApp.GetMathUtility();
			var startCorner = mMathUtility.CreatePoint(0,0,0);
			var oppositeCorner = mMathUtility.CreatePoint(3,3,0);

			//Create a rectangular Viewport
			var mViewport = mSheet.InsertRectangularViewport("dsStandardViewports_1", false, startCorner, oppositeCorner);

			//Activate and access the rectangular Viewport
			mViewport.put_Active = true;
			printOut(sheetName + ":");
			printOut("  Viewport:");
			printOut("    Height:                              " + mViewport.get_Height());
			printOut("    Width:                               " + mViewport.get_Width());
			printOut("    Locked in model workspace?           " + mViewport.get_DisplayLocked());
			printOut("    Displayed in graphics area?          " + mViewport.get_IsOn());
			printOut("    Visible?                             " + mViewport.get_Visible());

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <!-- Change the title so that it describes what the example demonstrates -->
    <title>Create Rectangular Viewport Example (JS)</title>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
            	document.getElementById("OB").value = "";
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

            //User input values
            var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X").value);
            var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y").value);
            var X0 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X0").value);
            var Y0 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y0").value);

            //Create the TableStyle
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();

            //Get how transparency is defined
            var mLayerManager = mDoc.GetLayerManager();
            var mLayer = mLayerManager.GetActiveLayer();
            var percentage = mLayer.get_Transparency();
            printOut("Transparency percentage for active Layer: " + JSON.stringify(percentage));

            //Set new transparency percentage for the active Layer
            var newPercentage = 50;
            mLayer.put_Transparency(newPercentage);
            var newPercentage = mLayer.get_Transparency();
            printOut("New transparency percentage for active Layer: " + JSON.stringify(newPercentage));

            var mTableStyleManager = mDoc.GetTableStyleManager();
            var sampleTableStyle = "Sample Table Style";
            var collTableStyle = mTableStyleManager.CreateTableStyle(sampleTableStyle);

            //Set the TableStyle styles
            //Create the Table in a downward direction
            collTableStyle.TableStyle.put_HeaderOrientation("dsTableHeaderOrientation_Down");

            //Set the Colors for the Table
            var mColorHeader = collTableStyle.TableStyle.GetBackgroundColor("dsTableCellType_Header");
        	mColorHeader.SetNamedColor("dsNamedColor_Cyan");
        	collTableStyle.TableStyle.SetBackgroundColor("dsTableCellType_Header", mColorHeader);

        	var mColorData = collTableStyle.TableStyle.GetBackgroundColor("dsTableCellType_Data");
			mColorData.SetNamedColor("dsNamedColor_Green");
		    collTableStyle.TableStyle.SetBackgroundColor("dsTableCellType_Data", mColorData);

			var mColorTitle = collTableStyle.TableStyle.GetBackgroundColor("dsTableCellType_Title");
			mColorTitle.SetNamedColor("dsNamedColor_White");
			collTableStyle.TableStyle.SetBackgroundColor("dsTableCellType_Title", mColorTitle);

            //Activate the TableStyle
			collTableStyle.TableStyle.Activate();

			//Get the model document
			var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();

			//Get the Sketch Manager
			var mSketchManager = mModel.GetSketchManager();

			//Insert the Table
			var rowHeight = 2;
			var colWidth = 4;
			var mTable = mSketchManager.InsertTable(X, Y, X0, Y0, rowHeight, colWidth, "dsTableCellType_Title", "dsTableCellType_Header", "dsTableCellType_Data");

			//Get transparency object for Table
			var mTransparency = mTable.get_Transparency();
			//Find out if Table's transparency ByLayer
			printOut("Transparency defined ByLayer: " + mTransparency.IsByLayer());
			//If not, then change Table's transparency to ByLayer and
			//update Table's transparency object
			if (mTransparency.IsByLayer() != true){
				mTransparency.SetByLayer();
				printOut("Transparency now ByLayer: " + dsTransparency.IsByLayer());
				//Update Table's transparency object
				dsTable.put_Transparency(dsTransparency);
			}

			//Change Table's top row's background color
			mColorTitle.SetNamedColor("dsNamedColor_Yellow");
			mTable.SetBackgroundColor("dsTableCellType_Title", mColorTitle);
			//Change background color of cell in second roww and second column
			//to match the top ro's background color
			mTable.SetCellBackgroundColor(1, 1, mColorTitle);

            printOut("Name of TableStyle: " + collTableStyle.TableStyle.get_Name());

            //Zoom to fit
            var lowerLeftArray = [2,0,0]
            var upperRightArray = [0,0,0]
            mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray)

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <!-- Change the title so that it describes what the example demonstrates -->
    <title>Create Table Example (JS)</title>

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,EntityGroups">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,groups">

	<head>

	<!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->

	<!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplicationOptions.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsColor.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsGroup.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLine.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsEntityHelper.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>

	<!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API -->
	<script type="text/javascript">
		function printOut(str) {
			document.getElementById("OB").value += str;
			document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
		}

		function runMacro() {
			//This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
			//It displays an error in the output box
			try {
				apiCode();
			}
			catch (err) {
				printOut(djErrorString(err));
			}
		}
		function apiCode() {
			var mScript = new djScriptManager();
			var dsApp = mScript.getApplication();
			dsApp.AbortRunningCommand();

			//Type your JavaScript API here

			//Insert entities

			var dsDoc = dsApp.GetActiveDocument();
			var dsModel = dsDoc.GetModel();
			var dsSketchMgr = dsModel.GetSketchManager();
			var line1 = dsSketchMgr.InsertLine(0, 0, 0, 10, 10, 0);
			var line2 = dsSketchMgr.InsertLine(5, 0, 0, 15, 10, 0);
			var circle1 = dsSketchMgr.InsertCircle(5, 5, 0, 10);
			var circle2 = dsSketchMgr.InsertCircle(10, 5, 0, 10);

			var EntitiesArray1 = Array(line1, line2);
			var EntitiesArray2 = Array(circle1, circle2);

			var dsGroup1 = dsDoc.CreateGroup("SampleGroup1", false, "This is my first sample group.", EntitiesArray1);

			printOut("SampleGroup1 created.\n");

			var mEntities = dsGroup1.GetEntities();

			//Change color of lines from white to red
			var dsEntityHelper = dsApp.GetEntityHelper();
			var dsColor = dsApp.GetNamedColor("dsNamedColor_Red");

			for (n=0; n < mEntities.EntitiesArray.length; n++){
				dsEntityHelper.SetColor (mEntities.EntitiesArray[n], dsColor);
            }

            printOut("Line entities colors changed from white to red.\n");

			var dsGroup2 = dsDoc.CreateGroup("SampleGroup2", false, "This is my second sample group.", EntitiesArray2);

            printOut("SampleGroup2 created.\n");

			//Get SampleGroup2 and remove circle2
			var dsGroup2 = dsDoc.GetGroup("SampleGroup2");
			if(dsGroup2.HasEntity(circle2)){
				var index = dsGroup2.GetIndex(circle2);
				dsGroup2.RemoveEntityAt (index);
			}

            printOut("circle2 removed from SampleGroup2.\n");

			//Get SampleGroup1 and add circle1 at last position
			var dsGroup1 = dsDoc.GetGroup("SampleGroup1");
			var EntitiesArray = new Array();
			EntitiesArray[0] = circle1;
			var count = dsGroup1.GetEntitiesCount();
			dsGroup1.InsertEntitiesAt(count, EntitiesArray);

			var newCount = dsGroup1.GetEntitiesCount();

			//Move circle1 to second position (index 1)
			circleIndex = dsGroup1.GetIndex(circle1);

			dsGroup1.Reorder(circleIndex, 1, 1);
			circleIndex = dsGroup1.GetIndex(circle1);

            printOut("circle1 added to SampleGroup1, and entities within SampleGroup1 reordered.\n");

			//Explode SampleGroup2
			dsGroup2.Explode();

            printOut("SampleGroup2 exploded.\n");

			//Rename SampleGroup1 and change description
			dsGroup1.Rename ("SampleGroup");
			dsGroup1.put_Description("My sample group.");

            printOut("SampleGroup1 renamed SampleGroup and its description also changed.\n");

			dsApp.releaseAll();
		}
	</script>

<<< Create and Remove Dictionary Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Create_and_Remove_Dictionary_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>

<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,dictionaries">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,XRecords">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,extension dictionaries">

<head>

	<!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
	<meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

	<meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

	<!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDictionary.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsXRecord.js"></script>

	<!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API -->
	<script type="text/javascript">

			function printOut(str) {
				document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
				document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
			}

			function runMacro() {

				//This catches any errors and exits out of the code if caught
				//Errors are displayed in Output field of form
				try {
					apiCode();
				}
					catch (err) {
						printOut(djErrorString(err));
					}
				}

				function apiCode() {
					var mScript = new djScriptManager();
					var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
					mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
					var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
					var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
					var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
					var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X").value);
					var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y").value);
					var Z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z").value);
					var R = parseFloat(document.getElementById("R").value)

					//Construct Circle
					var mCircle = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X, Y, Z, R);

					var dict = document.getElementById("dict").value;
					var rec = document.getElementById("rec").value;
					var recdata = document.getElementById("recdata").value;

					//Create extension dictionary for Circle
					var mCircleDict = mCircle.CreateExtensionDictionary();

					printOut("Name of Circle's dictionary: " + JSON.stringify(dict) + "\n");

					//Add XRecord to Circle extension dictionary
					var mRec = mCircleDict.CreateXRecord(rec);

					printOut("Name of record added to " + JSON.stringify(dict) + ": " + JSON.stringify(rec) + "\n");

					//Add data to XRecord
					mRec.InsertStringData(0, 1, recdata);
					var count = mRec.GetDataCount();
					var strData = mRec.GetStringData(count-1);
					printOut("Data added to " + JSON.stringify(rec) + " in " + JSON.stringify(dict) + ": " + JSON.stringify(strData) + "\n");

					//Remove XRecord from extension dictionary
					mCircleDict.RemoveEntry(rec);
					printOut(JSON.stringify(rec) + " removed from " + JSON.stringify(dict) + "\n");

					//Release and erase the extension dictionary
					var removed = mCircle.ReleaseExtensionDictionary();
					if (removed){
						printOut(JSON.stringify(dict) + " released and removed.");
					}
					mApp.releaseAll();
				}

</script>

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>

    <head>
    <meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSweep.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSweptSurface.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsBlockInstance.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsBlockDefinition.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            try {

                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {

            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mViewMgr = mDoc.GetViewManager();

            mViewMgr.SetPredefinedView("dsPredefinedView_SWIsometric");

            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0];
            var upperRightArray = [0,0,0];
            var docPath = "c:\\temp";
            if (document.inputform.R2.checked){
                //Flat shot by inserting a block

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline];
                var extrudes = [];

                mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight( dsEntities, 20, 0, extrudes);
                var aBlock = mSkMgr.FlatShotByInsertAsBlock( 2.0, 22.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0);
                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.R3.checked) {
                //Flat shot by replacing "Test" block

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline];
                var extrudes = [];

                mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight( dsEntities, 20, 0, extrudes);

                var dsCircle = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(23.0, 5.0, 0.0, 5.0);
                var dsEntities1 = [dsCircle];
                var dsEntityTypes = ["dsCircleType"];
                var dsBlockDefinition = mDoc.CreateBlockDefinition("Test", "", 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, dsEntityTypes, dsEntities1, "dsBlockDefinitionEntities_RemoveFromDrawing");
                var dsBlockInstance = mSkMgr.InsertBlock2("Test", -1.075, 14.997, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0);
                mSkMgr.FlatShotByReplaceExistingBlock ("Test");

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R4.checked) {
                //Flat shot exported to a file

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline];
                var extrudes = [];

                mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight( dsEntities, 20, 0, extrudes);

                mSkMgr.FlatShotByExportToFile (docPath + "\\" + "newFile.dwg");
                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);
            }

            else {
                //No selection
            	printOut("Please select an option.");
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            try {

                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {

            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mViewMgr = mDoc.GetViewManager();

            mViewMgr.SetPredefinedView("dsPredefinedView_SWIsometric");

            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0];
            var upperRightArray = [0,0,0];
            if (document.inputform.R2.checked){
                //Polysolid by entities

                var coordinates = [0.8839,7.3348,0.8839,6.0943,9.3647,6.0943,9.3647,7.3348];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline];

                var ds3DSolid = mSkMgr.PolysolidByEntities( dsEntities, 4.0, 0.25, "dsPolySolidJustification_Left");
                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.R3.checked) {
                //Polysolid by coordinates

                var coordinatesArray = [0.6339,3.9955,9.3647,3.9955];
                var ds3DSolid = mSkMgr.PolysolidByCoordinates( coordinatesArray, 4.0, 0.25, "dsPolySolidJustification_Left");
                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else {
                //No selection
            	printOut("Please select an option.");
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>

    <head>
    <meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSolid3D.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLoft.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLoftedSurface.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLine.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsColor.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsRevolve.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsRevolvedSurface.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathPoint.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathUtility.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircleArc.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsRegion.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }
        function runMacro() {
            try {

                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mViewMgr = mDoc.GetViewManager();

            mViewMgr.SetPredefinedView("dsPredefinedView_SWIsometric");

            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var dsMathUtility = mApp.GetMathUtility();
            var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0];
            var upperRightArray = [0,0,0];
            if (document.inputform.R2.checked){
                //Cross-section entities by 3 points

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                mPolyline.put_Elevation(100.0);
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline];
                var extrudes = [];
                extrudes = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight(dsEntities, 50, 0);
                var extrusions = [];
                extrusions = extrudes.ExtrusionArray;

                var FirstPoint = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(307.848, 166.459, 150.0);
                var SecondPoint = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(307.848, 166.459, 100.0);
                var ThirdPoint = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(128.253, 43.84, 150.0);
                var regions = [];

                regions = mSkMgr.CrossSectionEntitiesBy3Points(extrusions, FirstPoint, SecondPoint, ThirdPoint);
                var dsColor = mApp.GetColorByIndex(1);
                regions.RegionsArray[0].put_Color(dsColor);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.R3.checked) {
                //Cross-section entities by planar entity

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                mPolyline.put_Elevation(100.0);
                var mCircleArc = mSkMgr.InsertArc(218.0505, 105.1495, 100.0, 108.731070980194, 0.5990566759191, 3.74064932950889);
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline];
                var extrudes1 = [];
                extrudes1 = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight( dsEntities, 50.0, 0);
                var extrusions = [];
                extrusions = extrudes1.ExtrusionArray;

                var dsEntityPlanar = [mCircleArc];
                var regions = [];

                regions = mSkMgr.CrossSectionEntitiesByEntity( extrusions, dsEntityPlanar);
                var dsColor = mApp.GetColorByIndex(2);
                regions.RegionsArray[0].put_Color(dsColor);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R5.checked) {
                //Cross-section entities by view

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                mPolyline.put_Elevation(100.0);
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline];
                var extrudes7 = [];
                extrudes7 = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight (dsEntities, 50.0, 0);
                var extrusions = [];
                extrusions = extrudes7.ExtrusionArray;

                var regions = [];
                var PointOnActiveViewPlane = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(128.253, 43.84, 100.0);

                regions = mSkMgr.CrossSectionEntitiesByView( extrusions, PointOnActiveViewPlane);
                var dsColor = mApp.GetColorByIndex(3);
                regions.RegionsArray[0].put_Color(dsColor);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R6.checked) {
                //Cross-section entities by XY-plane

                var coordinates = [515.202,23.36,515.202,-82.68,367.471,-82.68,367.471,23.36];
                var mPolyline1 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var extrudes3 = [];
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline1];
                extrudes3 = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight( dsEntities, 50.0, 0);
                var extrusions = [];
                extrusions = extrudes3.ExtrusionArray;
                var regions = [];
                var PointOnActiveViewPlane2 = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(515.202, -82.68, 50.0);

                regions = mSkMgr.CrossSectionEntitiesByXY( extrusions, PointOnActiveViewPlane2);
                var dsColor = mApp.GetColorByIndex(5);
                regions.RegionsArray[0].put_Color(dsColor);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R7.checked) {
                //Cross-section entities by YZ-plane

                var coordinates = [515.202,23.36,515.202,-82.68,367.471,-82.68,367.471,23.36];
                var mPolyline1 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var extrudes4 = [];
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline1];
                extrudes4 = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight( dsEntities, 50.0, 0);
                var extrusions = [];
                extrusions = extrudes4.ExtrusionArray;
                var regions = [];
                var PointOnActiveViewPlane2 = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(515.202, -82.68, 50.0);

                regions = mSkMgr.CrossSectionEntitiesByYZ (extrusions, PointOnActiveViewPlane2);
                var dsColor = mApp.GetColorByIndex(6);
                regions.RegionsArray[0].put_Color(dsColor);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R8.checked) {
                //Cross-section entities by ZX-plane

                var coordinates = [515.202,23.36,515.202,-82.68,367.471,-82.68,367.471,23.36];
                var mPolyline1 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var extrudes5 = [];
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline1];
                extrudes5 = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight (dsEntities, 50.0, 0);
                var extrusions = [];
                extrusions = extrudes5.ExtrusionArray;
                var regions = [];
                var PointOnActiveViewPlane2 = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(515.202, -82.68, 50.0);

                regions = mSkMgr.CrossSectionEntitiesByZX( extrusions, PointOnActiveViewPlane2);
                var dsColor = mApp.GetColorByIndex(3);
                regions.RegionsArray[0].put_Color(dsColor);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R9.checked) {
                //Cross-section entities by Z-axis

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                mPolyline.put_Elevation(100.0);
                var dsEntities = [mPolyline];
                var extrudes6 = [];
                extrudes6 = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight(dsEntities, 50, 0);
                var extrusions = [];
                extrusions = extrudes6.ExtrusionArray;

                var regions = [];
                var FirstPoint = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(307.848, 166.459, 150.0);
                var SecondPoint = dsMathUtility.CreatePoint(307.848, 166.459, 100.0);

                regions = mSkMgr.CrossSectionEntitiesByZAxis(extrusions, FirstPoint, SecondPoint);
                var dsColor = mApp.GetColorByIndex(3);
                regions.RegionsArray[0].put_Color(dsColor);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else {
                //No selection
            	printOut("Please select an option.");
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }
        function runMacro() {
            try {

                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mViewMgr = mDoc.GetViewManager();

            mViewMgr.SetPredefinedView("dsPredefinedView_SWIsometric");

            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0];
            var upperRightArray = [0,0,0];
            if (document.inputform.R2.checked){
                //Extrude entities to solid by height

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var mCircle = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(221.876139410188, 159.343431635389, 0.0, 103.399785372141);

                var mEntities = [mPolyline, mCircle];
                var extrudes = [];

                extrudes = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByHeight( mEntities, 20.0, 0.0);
                var extrusion = extrudes.ExtrusionArray[0];
                extrusion.put_Height(30);
                var dsColor = mApp.GetColorByIndex(2);
                extrusion.put_Color(dsColor);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.R3.checked) {
                //Extrude entities to surface by height

                var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(550.0, 122.0, 0.0, 431.0, 70.0, 0.0);
                var mEntities = [mLine];
                var extrudes = [];

                extrudes = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSurfaceByHeight( mEntities, 20.0, 0.0);
                var extrudedSurface = extrudes.ExtrudedSurfaceArray[0];
                extrudedSurface.put_Height(50);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R4.checked) {
                //Extrude entities to solid by path

                var coordinates = [515.202,23.36,515.202,-82.68,367.471,-82.68,367.471,23.36];
                var mPolyline1 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(734.317, 74.52, 0.0, 734.317, 74.52, -309.475);
                var mEntities = [mPolyline1];
                var mPathEntities = [mLine];
                var extrudes = [];

                extrudes = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSolidByPath( mEntities, mPathEntities, 0.0);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R5.checked) {
                //Extrude entities to surface by path

                var coordinates = [659.732,89.273,659.732,16.042,555.531,16.042,555.531,89.273];
                var mPolyline1 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(734.317, 74.52, 0.0, 734.317, 74.52, -309.475);
                var mEntities3 = [mPolyline1];
                var mPathEntities = [mLine];
                var extrudes = [];

                extrudes = mSkMgr.ExtrudeEntitiesToSurfaceByPath (mEntities3, mPathEntities, 0.0);

                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }

            else {
                //No selection
            	printOut("Please select an option.");
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<<< Fillet and Stretch Sketch Entities Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Fillet_and_Stretch_Sketch_Entities_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples, fillet entities">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples, stretch entities">

    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCommandMessage.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsPolyLine.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircleArc.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLine.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                document.getElementById("OB").value = "";
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand()
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
            var choice;

           if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
                var firstPointOnEntityArray = new Array();
                var firstEntityArray = new Array();
                var secondPointOnEntityArray = new Array();
                var secondEntityArray = new Array();

                // Draw entities to fillet
            	var entityIndex = 0;
            	var coordinateIndex = 0;

            	// Draw a Line
            	var startPoint = new Array();
            	startPoint[0] = 0;
            	startPoint[1] = 0;
            	startPoint[2] = 0;
            	var endPoint = new Array();
            	endPoint[0] = 5;
            	endPoint[1] = 0;
            	endPoint[2] = 0;
            	var dsLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(startPoint[0], startPoint[1], startPoint[2], endPoint[0], endPoint[1], endPoint[2]);

            	// Add Line to output arrays
            	firstEntityArray[entityIndex] = dsLine;
            	firstPointOnEntityArray[coordinateIndex] = endPoint[0];
            	firstPointOnEntityArray[coordinateIndex + 1] = endPoint[1];
            	firstPointOnEntityArray[coordinateIndex + 2] = endPoint[2];

            	// Draw an Arc
            	var radius = 1;
            	var centerPoint = new Array();
            	centerPoint[0] = 2;
            	centerPoint[1] = -1;
            	centerPoint[2] = 0;
            	var startAngle = 3.14;
            	var endAngle = 1.5 * startAngle;
            	var dsArc = mSkMgr.InsertArc(centerPoint[0], centerPoint[1], centerPoint[2], radius, startAngle, endAngle);

            	// Add Arc to output arrays
            	secondEntityArray[entityIndex] = dsArc;
            	secondPointOnEntityArray[coordinateIndex] = centerPoint[0] - radius;
            	secondPointOnEntityArray[coordinateIndex + 1] = centerPoint[1];
            	secondPointOnEntityArray[coordinateIndex + 2] = centerPoint[2];

            	// Set fillet radius
            	var setResult = mDoc.SetCommandOptionDouble("dsCommandOptionDouble_SetFltRad", 1);
            	mSkMgr.FilletEntities(firstPointOnEntityArray, firstEntityArray, secondPointOnEntityArray, secondEntityArray);

            	var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0];
            	var upperRightArray = [0,0,0];
            	mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var displacementX = 4.0;
                var displacementY = 6.0;
                var displacementZ = 0.0;
                var dsEntities = new Array();
                var crossingBoxStartCorner = new Array();
                var crossingBoxEndCorner = new Array();

                // Draw entities to stretch
                var entitiesIndex = 0;

                // Draw PolyLine
                var closed = true;
                var coordinates = new Array();
                coordinates[0] = 1;
                coordinates[1] = 1;
				coordinates[2] = 3;
				coordinates[3] = 1;
				coordinates[4] = 3;
				coordinates[5] = 3;
				coordinates[6] = 1;
				coordinates[7] = 3;
            	var dsPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, closed);
            	// Add PolyLine to output array of entities
            	dsEntities[entitiesIndex] = dsPolyline;
            	entitiesIndex = entitiesIndex + 1;

            	// Draw Line
            	var startPoint = new Array();
            	startPoint[0] = 4;
            	startPoint[1] = 1;
				startPoint[2] = 0;
				var endPoint = new Array();
				endPoint[0] = 4;
				endPoint[1] = 5;
				endPoint[2] = 0;
            	var dsLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(startPoint[0], startPoint[1], startPoint[2], endPoint[0], endPoint[1], endPoint[2]);
            	// Add Line to output array of entities
            	dsEntities[entitiesIndex] = dsLine;
            	entitiesIndex = entitiesIndex + 1;

            	// Draw Circle
            	var radius = 1;
            	var centerPoint = new Array();
            	centerPoint[0] = 6;
            	centerPoint[1] = 2;
				centerPoint[2] = 0;

            	var dsCircle = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(centerPoint[0], centerPoint[1], centerPoint[2], radius);
            	// Add Circle to output array of entities
            	dsEntities[entitiesIndex] = dsCircle;
            	entitiesIndex = entitiesIndex + 1;

            	crossingBoxStartCorner[0] = 0.6;
            	crossingBoxStartCorner[1] = 2;
            	crossingBoxStartCorner[2] = 0;
            	crossingBoxEndCorner[0] = 8;
            	crossingBoxEndCorner[1] = 6;
            	crossingBoxEndCorner[2] = 0;

            	mSkMgr.StretchEntities(displacementX, displacementY, displacementZ, dsEntities, crossingBoxStartCorner, crossingBoxEndCorner);

            	var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0];
            	var upperRightArray = [0,0,0];
            	mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
