    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str;
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }

        function PrintDimensionStyleOptions(dsDimStyle) {
        	//Angular DimensionStyle options
        	var dsDimStyleAngularDimOptions  = dsDimStyle.GetDimensionStyleAngularDimensionOptions();
    		printOut("  Angular DimensionStyle options:  \n");
    		printOut("    Precision: " + dsDimStyleAngularDimOptions.get_Precision() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Format: " + dsDimStyleAngularDimOptions.get_Format() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide leading zeroes: " + dsDimStyleAngularDimOptions.get_HideLeadingZeroes() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide trailing zeroes: " + dsDimStyleAngularDimOptions.get_HideTrailingZeroes() + "\n");

    		//Dual DimensionStyle options
        	var dsDimStyleDualDimOptions  = dsDimStyle.GetDimensionStyleDualDimensionOptions();
    		printOut("  Dual DimensionStyle options:  \n");
    		printOut("    Precision: " + dsDimStyleDualDimOptions  .get_Precision() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Format: " + dsDimStyleDualDimOptions  .get_Format() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide leading zeroes: " + dsDimStyleDualDimOptions  .get_HideLeadingZeroes() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide trailing zeroes: " + dsDimStyleDualDimOptions  .get_HideTrailingZeroes() + "\n");

    		//Fit DimensionStyle options
        	var dsDimStyleFitOptions  = dsDimStyle.GetDimensionStyleFitOptions();
    		printOut("  Fit DimensionStyle options:  \n");
    		printOut("    Placement of text: " + dsDimStyleFitOptions.get_FitDimensionText() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Entity to move outside Dimension's extension lines: " + dsDimStyleFitOptions.get_FitGeometry() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide arrows: " + dsDimStyleFitOptions.get_HideArrows() + "\n");
    		var scales = dsDimStyleFitOptions.GetDimensionScale();
    		printOut("    Scale factor: " + scales.ScaleFactor + "\n");

    		//Linear DimensionStyle options
        	var dsDimStyleLinearDimOptions  = dsDimStyle.GetDimensionStyleLinearDimensionOptions();
    		printOut("  Linear DimensionStyle options:  \n");
    		printOut("    Precision: " + dsDimStyleLinearDimOptions.get_Precision() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Format: " + dsDimStyleLinearDimOptions.get_Format() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide leading zeroes: " + dsDimStyleLinearDimOptions.get_HideLeadingZeroes() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide trailing zeroes: " + dsDimStyleLinearDimOptions.get_HideTrailingZeroes() + "\n");

        	//Tolerance DimensionStyle options
        	var dsDimStyleToleranceOptions  = dsDimStyle.GetDimensionStyleToleranceOptions();
    		printOut("  Tolerance DimensionStyle options:  \n");
    		printOut("    Precision: " + dsDimStyleToleranceOptions.get_Precision() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Minimum value: " + dsDimStyleToleranceOptions.get_MinimumValue() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Maximum value: " + dsDimStyleToleranceOptions.get_MaximumValue() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide leading zeroes: " + dsDimStyleToleranceOptions.get_HideLeadingZeroes() + "\n");
    		printOut("    Hide trailing zeroes: " + dsDimStyleToleranceOptions.get_HideTrailingZeroes() + "\n");

		}

        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // Abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

            // Open document
            var mDoc = mApp.OpenDocument2("C:\\ProgramData\\Dassault Systemes\\DraftSight\\Examples\\B-44563.DWG", "dsDocumentOpen_Default", "dsEncoding_Default");

			if (mDoc == null){
				printOut("Specified document was not opened. Please check the path and file name.")

			}else{

                //Get the document's DimensionStyles
				var dsDimStyleMgr = mDoc.GetDimensionStyleManager();
				var dsDimStyles = dsDimStyleMgr.GetDimensionStyles();

				for (var i in dsDimStyles){
					var dsDimStyle = dsDimStyles[i]
					var dimStyleName = dsDimStyle.get_Name();
					printOut(dimStyleName + "\n");

					//Print each DimensionStyle's options to Output
					PrintDimensionStyleOptions(dsDimStyle);
				}

                //Export the document as PNG file
                var exportDoc = mDoc.GetDocumentExporter();
                var status = exportDoc.ExportToPng("C:\\ProgramData\\Dassault Systemes\\DraftSight\\Examples\\B-44563.PNG");

                if (status){
                	printOut("Drawing successfully exported to PNG file.");

                }else{

                	printOut("Export of drwing to PNG failed!");
                }

			}

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<<< Get Distance Between Two Math Points Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Get_Distance_Between_Two_Math_Points_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,math points">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,math utility">

    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathPoint.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsMathUtility.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
            	document.getElementById("OB").value = "";
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mMathUtil = mApp.GetMathUtility();
            var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X").value);
            var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y").value);
            var Z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z").value);
            var X0 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X0").value);
            var Y0 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y0").value);
            var Z0 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z0").value);
            var mMathPt1 = mMathUtil.CreatePoint(X, Y, Z);
            var mMathPt2 = mMathUtil.CreatePoint(X0, Y0, Z0);
            var distance = mMathUtil.Distance(mMathPt1, mMathPt2).Result;
            printOut("Distance between points: " + distance);
            var resultPt1 = mMathUtil.Distance(mMathPt1, mMathPt2).Point1;
            var resultPt2 = mMathUtil.Distance(mMathPt1, mMathPt2).Point2;
			printOut("First point position: " + resultPt1.GetPosition().X + ", " + resultPt1.GetPosition().Y + ", " + resultPt1.GetPosition().Z);
			printOut("Second point position: " + resultPt2.GetPosition().X + ", " + resultPt2.GetPosition().Y + ", " + resultPt2.GetPosition().Z);
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <!-- Change the title so that it describes what the example demonstrates -->
    <title>Get Distance Between Two Math Points Example (JS)</title>

            }

     //Type your JavaScript API code here
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            //Abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            //Get Print Manager
            var mPrintMgr = mApp.GetPrintManager();
            //Get available printers
            var printerArray = new Array() ;
            printerArray = mPrintMgr.GetAvailablePrinters();
            printOut("Printers available:");
            if (printerArray != null){
	            for(var i=0; i < printerArray.length;i++){
	            	printOut(printerArray[i]);
	            	alert(typeof printerArray[i]);
	            }
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }

			    document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
			    document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
			}

			function runMacro() {

			    //This catches any errors and exits out of the code if caught
			    //Errors are displayed in Output field of form

			   		apiCode();
			    }
			        catch (err) {
			            printOut(djErrorString(err));
			        }
				}

			function apiCode() {
			    var mScript = new djScriptManager();
			    var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
			    var mSystemFiles = mApp.GetSystemFiles();

			    mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

                //Print out system files
 	            printOut("System files:\n");

 	            printOut("  Help file:\n");
 	            printOut("    " + mSystemFiles.get_HelpFile() + "\n");

			    mApp.releaseAll();
			}

<<< Insert Block, Notes, and Sketch Entities Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Insert_Block_Notes_and_Sketch_Entitites_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Block definitions">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,2D PolyLines">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,rotate entities">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,documents">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,command window">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Sketch Manager">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Notes">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Circles">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,application">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,models">
    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCommandMessage.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsNote.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsBlockDefinition.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsBlockInstance.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsPolyLine.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
                //This is for the "Type coordinates" option
                //You must add text boxes in the HTML file to implement this option
                var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X").value);
                var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y").value);
                var Z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z").value);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var dsPoint = mCom.PromptForPoint("Insert at point in drawing");
                //This is for the "Insert at point in drawing" option
                //You must assign dsPoint.X, dsPoint.Y, and dsPoint.Z to the same variables
                //that are assigned in the "Type coordinates" option
                var X = dsPoint.X;
                var Y = dsPoint.Y;
                var Z = dsPoint.Z;
            }
            var checkBlock = mDoc.GetBlockDefinitions();
            var useExist = 0;
            for (var i in checkBlock) {
                if (checkBlock[i].GetName() == "Compass"){
                    useExist = 1;
                }
            }
            if (useExist == 1) {
                var block = mSkMgr.InsertBlock2("Compass", X, Y, Z, 1, 1, 1, 0);
            } else {
                var narray = new Array();
                var sarray = new Array();
                var earray = new Array();
                var warray = new Array();
                narray[0] = "N";
                sarray[0] = "S";
                earray[0] = "E";
                warray[0] = "W";
                var north = mSkMgr.InsertNoteWithParameters(X, Y + 4, 0, X + 5, Y + 4, 0, narray, 0, 1, "dsNoteJustification_MiddleCenter", "dsNoteLineSpacingStyle_AtLeast", 1, "Standard", 10);
                var south = mSkMgr.InsertNoteWithParameters(X, Y - 4, 0, X + 5, Y - 4, 0, sarray, 0, 1, "dsNoteJustification_MiddleCenter", "dsNoteLineSpacingStyle_AtLeast", 1, "Standard", 10);
                var west = mSkMgr.InsertNoteWithParameters(X - 4, Y, 0, X, Y, 0, warray, 0, 1, "dsNoteJustification_MiddleCenter", "dsNoteLineSpacingStyle_AtLeast", 1, "Standard", 10);
                var east = mSkMgr.InsertNoteWithParameters(X + 4, Y, 0, X + 6, Y, 0, earray, 0, 1, "dsNoteJustification_MiddleCenter", "dsNoteLineSpacingStyle_AtLeast", 1, "Standard", 10);
                var circle = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X, Y, Z, 5);
                var longarray = new Array();
                var entityarray = new Array();
                longarray[0] = 30;
                longarray[1] = 30;
                longarray[2] = 30;
                longarray[3] = 30;
                longarray[4] = 22;
                entityarray[0] = north;
                entityarray[1] = south;
                entityarray[2] = west;
                entityarray[3] = east;
                entityarray[4] = circle;
                var block = mDoc.CreateBlockDefinition("Compass", "This contains the shape of the compass", X, Y, Z, longarray, entityarray, "dsBlockDefinitionEntities_ConvertToBlock");
            }
            //Find a way to get the arrow to revolve around
            var direction = parseFloat(document.getElementById("D").value);
            var arrowarray = new Array();
            arrowarray[0] = X - 0.5;
            arrowarray[1] = Y - 1;
            arrowarray[2] = X + 0.5;
            arrowarray[3] = Y - 1;
            arrowarray[4] = X + 0.5;
            arrowarray[5] = Y + 1;
            arrowarray[6] = X + 1;
            arrowarray[7] = Y + 1;
            arrowarray[8] = X;
            arrowarray[9] = Y + 2;
            arrowarray[10] = X - 1;
            arrowarray[11] = Y + 1;
            arrowarray[12] = X - 0.5;
            arrowarray[13] = Y + 1;
            var arrow = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(arrowarray, true);
            var long2 = new Array();
            var entity2 = new Array();
            long2[0] = 27;
            entity2[0] = arrow;
            //mSkMgr.Mirror(X, Y + 2, 0, X, Y - 1, 0, false, long2, entity2);
            mSkMgr.RotateEntities(X, Y, direction, long2, entity2);
            //Type your JavaScript API code here
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,embedded objects">
    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsEmbeddedObject.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsColor.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLayer.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
            	document.getElementById("OB").value = "";
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var file = document.getElementById("File1").value;
            var link = true;
            var displayAsIcon = false;
            var dsEmbeddedObject = mSkMgr.InsertEmbeddedObjectFromFile(file, link, displayAsIcon);
            if (dsEmbeddedObject != null) {
            	var handle = dsEmbeddedObject.get_Handle();
            	printOut("Handle: " + handle);
        		var color = dsEmbeddedObject.get_Color();
				var namedColor = color.GetNamedColor();
				printOut("Color: " + namedColor);
        		var erased = dsEmbeddedObject.get_Erased();
				printOut("Erased? " + erased);
        		var layer = dsEmbeddedObject.get_Layer();
				printOut("Layer: " + layer);
        		var linescale = dsEmbeddedObject.get_LineScale();
				printOut("Line scale: " + linescale);
				var linestyle = dsEmbeddedObject.get_LineStyle();
				printOut("Line style: " + linestyle);
        		var lineweight = dsEmbeddedObject.get_LineWeight();
				printOut("Line weight: " + lineweight);
        		var visible = dsEmbeddedObject.get_Visible();
				printOut("Visible? " + visible);

        		var x1;
				var y1;
				var z1;
        		var x2;
				var y2;
				var z2;
        		var bout = dsEmbeddedObject.GetBoundingBox();
        		printOut("Opposite corners of bounding box: " + bout.X1 + "," +  bout.Y1 + "," + bout.Z1 + " and " +  bout.X2 + "," + bout.Y2 + "," + bout.Z2);

        		//Print embedded object parameters
        		var height = dsEmbeddedObject.get_Height();
				printOut("Height: " + height);
        		var width = dsEmbeddedObject.get_Width();
				printOut("Width: " + width);
        		var printquality = dsEmbeddedObject.get_PrintQuality();
				printOut("Print quality: " + printquality);
        		var linkpath = dsEmbeddedObject.GetLinkPath();
				printOut("Link path: " + linkpath);
       	 		var sourceapp = dsEmbeddedObject.GetSourceApplication();
				printOut("Source application: " + sourceapp);
        		var type = dsEmbeddedObject.GetType();
				printOut("Type of embedded object: " + type);

        		//Get position of embedded object
        		var x;
				var y;
				var z;
        		var pout = dsEmbeddedObject.GetPosition();
        		printOut("Position: " + pout.X + "," + pout.Y + "," + pout.Z);

			}
			else {
			 	printOut("File object was not embedded in the document.");
			}
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Hatches">
    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSelectionManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsPolyLine.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLine.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                document.getElementById("OB").value = "";
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mSelMgr = mDoc.GetSelectionManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
            	var X2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X2").value);
                var Y2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y2").value);
                var X4 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X4").value);
                var Y4 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y4").value);
                var X5 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X5").value);
                var Y5 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y5").value);
                var X6 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X6").value);
                var Y6 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y6").value);
                var bArray = new Array();
			    bArray[0] = X2;
			    bArray[1] = Y2;
			    bArray[2] = X4;
			    bArray[3] = Y4;
			    bArray[4] = X5;
			    bArray[5] = Y5;
			    bArray[6] = X6;
			    bArray[7] = Y6;
			    var longArray = new Array();
			    longArray[0] = 4;
			    var patName = document.getElementById("H1").value;
                var patScale = parseFloat(document.getElementById("H2").value);
				var patAngle = parseFloat(document.getElementById("H3").value);
                var hatch = mSMgr.InsertHatchByBoundary(longArray, bArray, patName, patScale, patAngle);
                var color = hatch.get_Color();
                printOut("Color of new Hatch: " + color.GetNamedColor());
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
            	var prompt = "Select a Hatch boundary entity"
            	var errorMessage = "Unknown entity."
            	var statusSelect = mCom.PromptForSelection(false, prompt, errorMessage);
            	if (statusSelect) {
            		var count = mSelMgr.GetSelectedObjectCount("dsSelectionSetType_Previous");
            		var i;
            		var dsEntityType;
            		var dsEntities = new Array();
            		printOut("Number of entities selected: " + count);
            		for (i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            			dsEntities[i] = mSelMgr.GetSelectedObject("dsSelectionSetType_Previous", i, dsEntityType).SelObj;
            		}
            		var patName = document.getElementById("H1").value;
                	var patScale = parseFloat(document.getElementById("H2").value);
					var patAngle = parseFloat(document.getElementById("H3").value);
                	var hatch = mSMgr.InsertHatchByEntities(dsEntities, patName, patScale, patAngle);
                	var color = hatch.get_Color();
                	printOut("Color of new Hatch: " + color.GetNamedColor());
                }
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[2].checked){
                var X7 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X7").value);
                var Y7 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y7").value);
                var X8 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X8").value);
                var Y8 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y8").value);
                var X9 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X9").value);
                var Y9 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y9").value);
                var X10 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X10").value);
                var Y10 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y10").value);
                var bArray = new Array();
			    bArray[0] = X7;
			    bArray[1] = Y7;
			    bArray[2] = X8;
			    bArray[3] = Y8;
			    bArray[4] = X9;
			    bArray[5] = Y9;
			    bArray[6] = X10;
			    bArray[7] = Y10;
                var patName = document.getElementById("H1").value;
                var patScale = parseFloat(document.getElementById("H2").value);
				var patAngle = parseFloat(document.getElementById("H3").value);
                var hatch = mSMgr.InsertHatchByInternalPoints(bArray, patName, patScale, patAngle);
                var color = hatch.get_Color();
                printOut("Color of new Hatch: " + color.GetNamedColor());

            }

           mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>

    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
                //This is for the "Type coordinates" option
                //You must add text boxes in the HTML file to implement the "Type coordinates" option
                var X1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X1").value);
                var Y1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y1").value);
                var Z1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z1").value);
                var X2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X2").value);
                var Y2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y2").value);
                var Z2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z2").value);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var dsPoint1 = mCom.PromptForPoint("Select where to place the upper-left corner:");
                //This is for the select a point in DraftSight
                //You must assign dsPoint.X, dsPoint.Y, and dsPoint.Z to the same variables
                //that are assigned in the "Type coordinates" option
                var X1 = dsPoint1.X;
                var Y1 = dsPoint1.Y;
                var Z1 = dsPoint1.Z;
                var dsPoint2 = mCom.PromptForPoint("Select where to place the lower-right corner:");
                var X2 = dsPoint2.X;
                var Y2 = dsPoint2.Y;
                var Z2 = dsPoint2.Z;
            }
            //Type your JavaScript API here
            var link = mSkMgr.AttachLinkToArea(X1, Y1, Z1, X2, Y2, Z2, "mailto:nobody@draftsight.com", "Send an email to nobody.");
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <H1>Insert HyperLink Example (JavaScript)</H1>

    <body>
        <table style="text-align: left; width: 860px; height: 600px; font-family: Verdana, sans-serif;" border="0"
            cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0">

            <tbody>
               <tr>
                    <td style="height: 55px">
                        <div class="style7">
							This example inserts a HyperLink, which you can use to
							send an email message from a DraftSight
							drawing. <ol>
                            <li>Start DraftSight.<li>Using the following form, specify where to place the HyperLink:</li>
                            <ol>
                            <li>Select <b>Coordinates</b>.</li>
                            <li>Type x1, y1, z1, x2, y2, and z2 coordinates.</li>
                            <li>Click <b>Insert</b>.</li>
                            <ul>- or -</ul>
                            </ol>
                            <ol>
                            <li>Select <b>Point in drawing.</b></li>
                            <li>Click <b>Insert</b>.</li>
                            <li>Click anywhere in the drawing to insert the HyperLink at that point.</li>
                            </ol>
                            <li>Examine the drawing and locate the HyperLink (i.e., place your cursor over a red line).</li>
                            <li>Ctrl-click the HyperLink to open the email message.</li>
							<li>Change the email address of the recipient.</li>

    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str;
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var doublearray = new Array();
            var xstring = prompt("Type the x coordinate for the dimension", "13");
            doublearray[0] = xstring;
            var ystring = prompt("Type the y coordinate for the dimension", "4");
            doublearray[1] = ystring;
            var zstring = prompt("Type the z coordinate for the dimension", "0");
            doublearray[2] = zstring;
            printOut(" X: " + xstring + " Y: " + ystring + " Z: " + zstring + "\n");

            /*For debugging purposes to see if it is placed into the array*/
            /*document.write(doublearray);*/

            /*Uncomment when it is ready to be sent to the server*/
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
                var SX1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("SX1").value);
                var SY1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("SY1").value);
                var SZ1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("SZ1").value);
                var EX1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("EX1").value);
                var EY1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("EY1").value);
                var EZ1 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("EZ1").value);
                var SX2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("SX2").value);
                var SY2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("SY2").value);
                var SZ2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("SZ2").value);
                var EX2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("EX2").value);
                var EY2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("EY2").value);
                var EZ2 = parseFloat(document.getElementById("EZ2").value);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var wiiPoint1 = mCom.PromptForPoint("Select where you want to start the first line");
                var SX1 = wiiPoint1.X;
                var SY1 = wiiPoint1.Y;
                var SZ1 = wiiPoint1.Z;
                var wiiPoint2 = mCom.PromptForPoint("Select where you want to end the first line");
                var EX1 = wiiPoint2.X;
                var EY1 = wiiPoint2.Y;
                var EZ1 = wiiPoint2.Z;
                var wiiPoint3 = mCom.PromptForPoint("Select where you want to start the second line");
                var SX2 = wiiPoint3.X;
                var SY2 = wiiPoint3.Y;
                var SZ2 = wiiPoint3.Z;
                var wiiPoint4 = mCom.PromptForPoint("Select where you want to end the second line");
                var EX2 = wiiPoint4.X;
                var EY2 = wiiPoint4.Y;
                var EZ2 = wiiPoint4.Z;
            }
            else {
                return false;
            }

            var OR = document.getElementById("OR").value;

            var mLine1 = mSkMgr.InsertLine(SX1, SY1, SZ1, EX1, EY1, EZ1);
            var mLine2 = mSkMgr.InsertLine(SX2, SY2, SZ2, EX2, EY2, EZ2);
            var mDim = mSkMgr.InsertAngularDimension2Line(mLine1, mLine2, doublearray, OR);
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

							This example constructs two lines and inserts an
							angular dimension between the lines in a DraftSight drawing.<ol>
                            <li>Start DraftSight.<li>Using the following form, specify the text for the angular dimension in <b>
							Dimension text</b>:<li>Specify where to place the lines:
                            <ol>
                            <li>Select <b>Coordinates</b>.</li>
                            <li>Type the x, y, and z coordinates for the start
							and end lines in <b>First line start</b>, <b>First
							line end</b>, <b>Second line start</b>, and <b>
							Second line end</b>.</li>
							<li>When prompted, type the x, y, and z coordinates
							for the angular dimension and click <b>OK</b> after each prompt.</li>
                            <li>Click <b>Insert</b>.</li>
                            <ul>- or -</ul>
                            </ol>
                            <ol>
                            <li>Select <b>Points in drawing.</b></li>
							<li>Click <b>Insert</b>.</li>
							<li>When prompted, type the x, y, and z coordinates
							for the angular dimension and click <b>OK</b> after each prompt.</li>
							<li>Click anywhere in the drawing to insert the
							start and end points for the first and second lines.</li>
                            </ol>
                            <li>Examine the drawing.</li>

<<< Insert Shapes Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Insert_Shapes_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,shapes">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Shape Manager">

	<head>

	<!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
	<meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

	<meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

	<!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsShape.js"></script>
	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsShapeManager.js"></script>

	<!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
	<script type="text/javascript">
		function printOut(str) {
			document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
			document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
		}

		function runMacro() {
			//This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
			//It displays an error in the output box
			try {
				apiCode();
			}
			catch (err) {
				printOut(djErrorString(err));
			}
		}
		function apiCode() {
			var mScript = new djScriptManager();
			var mApp = mScript.getApplication();

			//Abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
			mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

			//Get document, model, and Sketch Manager
			var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
			var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
			var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();

			//Get Shape Manager
			var mShapeManager = mDoc.GetShapeManager();

			//Load shape file and get shapes
			var shapeFile = "c:\\Program Files\\Dassault Systemes\\DraftSight\\Fonts\\LTypeShp.shx";
			mShapeManager.Load(shapeFile);
			var shapesStrList = mShapeManager.GetAvailableShapes();

			var shapesCount = 0;
			shapesCount = shapesStrList.length;

			//Insert shapes
			var PositionX = 0;
			var PositionY = 0;
			var PositionZ = 0;
			for (var i = 0; i < shapesCount; ++i) {
			    var mShape = mSkMgr.InsertShape(shapesStrList[i], PositionX, PositionY, PositionZ, 1, 0);
			    var angle = 30 * Math.PI / 180;
			    mShape.put_Obliquing(angle);
			    var X1 = 0;
			    var Y1 = 0;
			    var Z1 = 0;
			    var X2 = 0;
			    var Y2 = 0;
			    var Z2 = 0;

			    mShape.GetBoundingBox(X1, Y1, Z1, X2, Y2, Z2);

			    PositionX += (X2 - X1) + 5;

			}

			mApp.releaseAll();
		}
	</script>

        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str;
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();

            //Type your JavaScript API code here

            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
                var x = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T1").value);
                var y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T2").value);
                var z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T3").value);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var wiiPoint = mCom.PromptForPoint("Please select where the bottom left corner of the 3DS logo should go");
                var x = wiiPoint.X;
                var y = wiiPoint.Y;
                var z = wiiPoint.Z;
            }

                return false;

            //var y = 0;

            var spArray2 = new Array();
            var spArray3 = new Array();

            //This is for the D
            spArray1[0] = x + 0.4513;
            spArray1[1] = y + 0.3825;
            spArray1[2] = z + 0.0;

            spArray1[25] = y + 0.4568;

            var spline1 = mSkMgr.InsertSpline(spArray1, true, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);

            //This is for the S
            spArray2[0] = x + 0.4659;
            spArray2[1] = y + 0.1472;
            spArray2[2] = z + 0.0;
            spArray2[3] = x + 0.8218;
            spArray2[4] = y + 0.2052;

            spArray2[23] = z + 0.0;

            var spline2 = mSkMgr.InsertSpline(spArray2, true, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);

            //This is for the 3
            spArray3[0] = x + 0.6319;

            var realcount = 1;
            printOut("D Part\n");
            for (n = 0; n < 27; n = n + 3) {

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCommandMessage.js"></script>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsAlignedDimension.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsGeneralDimension.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDimensionStyle.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str;
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
            var bR = parseFloat(document.getElementById("bR").value);
            var sR = parseFloat(document.getElementById("sR").value);
            var L = parseFloat(document.getElementById("L").value);

            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
                var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T1").value);
                var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T2").value);
                var Z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("T3").value);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var wiiPoint = mCom.PromptForPoint("Please place the center point of the large Arc.");
                var X = wiiPoint.X;
                var Y = wiiPoint.Y;
                var Z = wiiPoint.Z;
            }
            else {
                return false;
            }

            var bRadius = bR/2;
            var sRadius = sR/2;
            //This will calculate the angles depending on the small radius
            var angle1 = Math.asin(sRadius/bRadius);
            var angle2 = 2*Math.PI - angle1;
            var bArc = mSkMgr.InsertArc(X, Y, Z, bRadius, angle1, angle2);

            printOut("Center of large Arc\n");
            printOut("X: " + X + " Y: " + Y + " Z: " + Z + "\n");

            var disLine = Math.cos(angle1)*bRadius;
            var stretch = L - disLine;
            //For the top line, line1, from left to right
            var x1 = X + disLine;
            var y1 = Y + sRadius;
            var z1 = Z;
            var x2 = X + disLine + stretch;
            var y2 = Y + sRadius;
            var z2 = Z;
            //For the bottom line, line2, from left to right
            var x3 = X + disLine;
            var y3 = Y - sRadius;
            var z3 = Z;
            var x4 = X + disLine + stretch;
            var y4 = Y - sRadius;
            var z4 = Z;
            var line1 = mSkMgr.InsertLine(x1, y1, z1, x2, y2, z2);
            var line2 = mSkMgr.InsertLine(x3, y3, z3, x4, y4, z4);
            var sArc = mSkMgr.InsertArc(X + L, Y, Z, sRadius, 4.71238898, 7.85398163);
            printOut("Center of small Arc\n");
            printOut("X: " + (X + L) + " Y: " + Y + " Z: " + Z + "\n");
            var dimArray1 = new Array();
            dimArray1[0] = X - 2*bRadius;
            dimArray1[1] = Y + 2*bRadius;
            dimArray1[2] = Z;

            //This is where the code tries to change the height of the dimension text
            var styleMgr = mDoc.GetDimensionStyleManager();
            var dimStyle = styleMgr.GetActiveDimensionStyle();
            var dimText1 = dimStyle.GetDimensionStyleTextOptions();
            dimText1.put_Height(1);
            var hh = dimText1.get_Height();

            var dim1 = mSkMgr.InsertDiameterDimensionArc(bArc, dimArray1, "");
            var dimArray2 = new Array();
            dimArray2[0] = X + L + 2*sRadius;
            dimArray2[1] = Y - 2*sRadius;
            dimArray2[2] = Z;
            var dim2 = mSkMgr.InsertDiameterDimensionArc(sArc, dimArray2, "");
            dimArray1[0] = X;
            dimArray1[1] = Y;
            dimArray1[2] = Z;
            dimArray2[0] = X + L;
            dimArray2[1] = Y;
            dimArray2[2] = Z;
            var dimArray3 = new Array();
            dimArray3[0] = X + L/2;
            dimArray3[1] = Y - 2*bRadius;
            dimArray3[2] = Z;
            var dim3 = mSkMgr.InsertAlignedDimension(dimArray1, dimArray2, dimArray3, "");
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

	<script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLineStyle.js"></script>

	<!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
	<script type="text/javascript">
		function printOut(str) {
			document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
			document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
		}

		function runMacro() {
			//This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
			//It displays an error in the output box
			try {
				apiCode();
			}
			catch (err) {
				printOut(djErrorString(err));
			}
		}
		function apiCode() {
			var mScript = new djScriptManager();
			var mApp = mScript.getApplication();

			//Abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
			mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

			//Get document, model, and Sketch Manager
			var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
			var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
			var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();

			//Get LineStyle Manager
			var mLineStyleManager = mDoc.GetLineStyleManager();

			//Load LineStyles
			var LineStyleFile = "c:\\Program Files\\Dassault Systemes\\DraftSight\\Default Files\\Linestyles\\MM.LIN";
			var mColl = mLineStyleManager.LoadLineStyle("BATTING", LineStyleFile);
			var mColl = mLineStyleManager.LoadLineStyle("ZIGZAG", LineStyleFile);

			var mLineStyle = mColl.LineStyle;

			//Construct Line using BATTING LineStyle
			mLineStyle = mLineStyleManager.GetLineStyle("BATTING");
			mLineStyle.Activate();
			var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(0, 0, 0, 100, 100, 0);

			//Constuct Line using ZIGZAG LineStle
			mLineStyle = mLineStyleManager.GetLineStyle("ZIGZAG");
			mLineStyle.Activate();
			var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(100, 0, 0, 200, 100, 0);

		   	mApp.releaseAll();
		}
	</script>

<<< Lofting Entities Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Lofting_Entities_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>

    <head>
    <meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
    <meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,lofts">

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" >
    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" >

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsExtrusion.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsViewManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsPolyLine.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSolid3D.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLoft.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLoftedSurface.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLine.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            try {

                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {

            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mViewMgr = mDoc.GetViewManager();

            mViewMgr.SetPredefinedView("dsPredefinedView_SWIsometric");

            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0];
            var upperRightArray = [0,0,0];
            if (document.inputform.R2.checked){
                //Loft entities to solid by guides

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                mPolyline.put_Elevation(100.0);
                var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(432.58, 113.544, 0.0, 432.58, 113.544, -155.752);
                var mCircle = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(221.876139410188, 159.343431635389, 0.0, 103.399785372141);

                var mEntities = [mPolyline, mCircle];
                var mGuideEntities = [mLine];

                var dsLoft = mSkMgr.LoftEntitiesToSolidByGuides(mEntities, false, mGuideEntities, "dsLoftSurfaceNormalOption_SmoothFit", "dsLoftedSurfaceNormalOption_SmoothFit", 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.R3.checked) {
                //Loft entities to surface by guides

                var coordinates = [128.253,166.459,128.253,43.84,307.848,43.84,307.848,166.459];
                var mPolyline = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                mPolyline.put_Elevation(100.0);
                var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(432.58, 113.544, 0.0, 432.58, 113.544, -155.752);
                var mCircle = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(221.876139410188, 159.343431635389, 0.0, 103.399785372141);
                var mEntities = [mPolyline, mCircle];
                var mGuideEntities = [mLine];

                var dsLoft = mSkMgr.LoftEntitiesToSurfaceByGuides(mEntities, false, mGuideEntities, "dsLoftSurfaceNormalOption_SmoothFit", "dsLoftedSurfaceNormalOption_SmoothFit", 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R4.checked) {
                //Loft entities to solid by path
                var coordinates = [486.181,78.416,486.181,246.617,675.147,246.617,675.147,78.416];
                var mPolyline1 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                mPolyline1.put_Elevation(100.0);
                var coordinates = [546.401,200.933,546.401,123.062,675.147,123.062,675.147,200.933];
                var mPolyline2 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(486.181, 246.617, 100.0, 546.401, 200.933, 0.0);
                var mEntities = [mPolyline1, mPolyline2];
                var mPathEntities = [mLine];

                var dsLoft = mSkMgr.LoftEntitiesToSolidByPath(mEntities, false, mPathEntities, "dsLoftSurfaceNormalOption_SmoothFit", "dsLoftedSurfaceNormalOption_SmoothFit", 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }
            else if (document.inputform.R5.checked) {
                //Loft entities to surface by path
                var coordinates = [486.181,78.416,486.181,246.617,675.147,246.617,675.147,78.416];
                var mPolyline1 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                mPolyline1.put_Elevation(100.0);
                var coordinates = [546.401,200.933,546.401,123.062,675.147,123.062,675.147,200.933];
                var mPolyline2 = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(coordinates, true);
                var mLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(486.181, 246.617, 100.0, 546.401, 200.933, 0.0);
                var mEntities = [mPolyline1, mPolyline2];
                var mPathEntities = [mLine];

                var dsLoft = mSkMgr.LoftEntitiesToSurfaceByPath(mEntities, false, mPathEntities, "dsLoftSurfaceNormalOption_SmoothFit", "dsLoftedSurfaceNormalOption_SmoothFit", 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
                mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray);

            }

            else {
                //No selection
            	printOut("Please select an option.");
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<<< Manage Custom Coordinate Systems Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Manage_Default_and_Named_Custom_Coordinate_Systems_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,custom coordinate systems">

    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsViewManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCustomCoordinateSystemManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCustomCoordinateSystem.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsLine.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            try {
            	document.getElementById("OB").value = "";
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function printCoordinateSystemParameters(dsCCS) {
        	printOut("Custom Coordinate System Parameters:");
        	printOut("Name: " + dsCCS.GetName());
        	var dsColl = dsCCS.GetDefaultCustomCoordinateSystemFlag();
        	printOut("IsDefault? " +  dsColl.IsDefault);
        	printOut("Default Custom Coordinate system flag: " + dsColl.DefaultCustomCoordinateSystemFlag);
        	var dsOrigin = dsCCS.GetOrigin();
        	printOut("Origin x coordinate: " + dsOrigin.OriginX);
        	printOut("Origin y coordinate: " + dsOrigin.OriginY);
        	printOut("Origin xz coordinate: " + dsOrigin.OriginXZ);
        	var dsXAxisDirection = dsCCS.GetXAxisDirection();
        	printOut("X axis direction x coordinate: " + dsXAxisDirection.X);
        	printOut("X axis direction y coordinate: " + dsXAxisDirection.Y);
        	printOut("X axis direction z coordinate: " + dsXAxisDirection.Z);
        	var dsYAxisDirection = dsCCS.GetYAxisDirection();
        	printOut("Y axis direction x coordinate: " + dsYAxisDirection.X);
        	printOut("Y axis direction y coordinate: " + dsYAxisDirection.Y);
        	printOut("Y axis direction z coordinate: " + dsYAxisDirection.Z);
        	var dsZAxisDirection = dsCCS.GetZAxisDirection();
        	printOut("Z axis direction x coordinate: " + dsZAxisDirection.X);
        	printOut("Z axis direction y coordinate: " + dsZAxisDirection.Y);
        	printOut("Z axis direction z coordinate: " + dsZAxisDirection.Z);
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var dsCCSManager = mDoc.GetCustomCoordinateSystemManager();
            var mViewMgr = mDoc.GetViewManager();
            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
            	// Get World CCS
            	printOut("Getting the World CCS...");
                var dsWorldCCS = dsCCSManager.GetWorldCustomCoordinateSystem();
                printCoordinateSystemParameters(dsWorldCCS);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
            	//Activate default Bottom CCS
            	printOut("Activating the default Bottom CCS...");
                var dsBottomCCS = dsCCSManager.GetDefaultCustomCoordinateSystem("dsDefaultCustomCoordinateSystem_Bottom");
                printCoordinateSystemParameters(dsBottomCCS);
                dsBottomCCS.Activate();
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[2].checked){
            	//Change origin of active CCS
            	printOut("Changing the origin of active CCS...");
            	var originX = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X1").value);
            	var originY = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y1").value);
            	var originZ = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z1").value);
            	dsCCSManager.SetActiveCustomCoordinateSystemOrigin(originX, originY, originZ);
            	var dsActiveCCS = dsCCSManager.GetActiveCustomCoordinateSystem();
            	printCoordinateSystemParameters(dsActiveCCS);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[3].checked){
            	//Align active CCS with a Line
            	printOut("Aligning the active CCS with a Line...");
            	var startPoint = new Array();
            	startPoint[0] = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X2").value);
            	startPoint[1] = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y2").value);
            	startPoint[2] = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z2").value);
            	var endPoint = new Array();
            	endPoint[0] = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X3").value);
            	endPoint[1] = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y3").value);
            	endPoint[2] = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z3").value);
            	var dsLine = mSkMgr.InsertLine(startPoint[0], startPoint[1], startPoint[2], endPoint[0], endPoint[1], endPoint[2]);
            	dsCCSManager.AlignActiveCustomCoordinateSystemByEntity(dsLine, startPoint);
            	var dsActiveCCS = dsCCSManager.GetActiveCustomCoordinateSystem();
            	printCoordinateSystemParameters(dsActiveCCS);
            }
			else if (document.inputform.Choose[4].checked){
				//Create a named CCS
				printOut("Creating a named CCS...");
            	mViewMgr.SetPredefinedView("dsPredefinedView_SWIsometric");
            	//mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", null, null);
            	var name = document.getElementById("newname").value;
            	var dsColl = dsCCSManager.SaveActiveViewAs(name);
            	var namedCCS = dsColl.CustomCoordinateSystem;
            	printCoordinateSystemParameters(namedCCS);
            	//Rename a CCS
            	printOut("Renaming a CCS...");
            	namedCCS.Rename(document.getElementById("nametorename").value);
            	printCoordinateSystemParameters(namedCCS);
            	// Print the list of named CCS's
            	printOut("List of named CCS's...");
            	var namedCCSList = new Array();
            	namedCCSList = dsCCSManager.GetNamedCustomCoordinateSystemList();
            	var count = namedCCSList.length;
            	var i;
            	for (i = 0; i<count; i++) {
            		namedCCS = namedCCSList[i];
            		printOut(namedCCS);
            	}
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[5].checked) {
            	//Remove a CCS
            	printOut("Removing a named CCS...");
            	var removename = document.getElementById("removename").value;
            	dsCCSManager.RemoveCustomCoordinateSystem(removename);
            	// Print the list of named CCS's (should be one less now)
            	printOut("Current list of named CCS's...");
            	var namedCCSList = new Array();
            	namedCCSList = dsCCSManager.GetNamedCustomCoordinateSystemList();
            	if (namedCCSList != null) {
            		var count = namedCCSList.length;
            		var i;
            		for (i = 0; i<count; i++) {
            			namedCCS = namedCCSList[i];
            			printOut(namedCCS);
            		}
            	}
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDSWindow.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str;
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            // abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
			var mDSWindow = mDoc.GetWindow();

            //Type your JavaScript API code here
            var windowState = mDSWindow.get_WindowState();
            if (windowState=="dsWindowState_Max"){
                mDSWindow.put_WindowState("dsWindowState_Min");
            }
            else if (windowState=="dsWindowState_Min"){
                mDSWindow.put_WindowState("dsWindowState_Max");
            }
            else if (windowState=="dsWindowState_Norm"){
            	mDSWindow.put_WindowState("dsWindowState_Min");
            }
            else {
                return false;
            }

            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

<<< Mirror Entities Example (JS) | draftsightapi/Mirror_Entities_Example_JS.md
<!DOCTYPE html PUBLIC "-//W3C//DTD HTML 4.01 Transitional//EN">
<html>
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,documents">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,command window">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Splines">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,application">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Sketch Manager">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,models">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,2D PolyLines">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,Arcs">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,mirror entities">
<meta name=MS-HKWD content="JavaScript examples,zoom">

    <head>

    <!-- Without this line, this does not work in Internet Explorer 8 -->
    <meta http-equiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=8" />

    <meta content="text/html; charset=ISO-8859-1" http-equiv="content-type" />

    <!-- Add your dsInterface .js Files here -->
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/djConnect.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsApplication.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsDocument.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsModel.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsSketchManager.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCommandMessage.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsPolyLine.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircleArc.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();
            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();
            var mCom = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
            if (document.inputform.Choose[0].checked){
                //This is for the "Enter Coordinates" option
                //You must add textboxes in the html file in order to implement this option
                var X = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X").value);
                var Y = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y").value);
                var Z = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z").value);
            }
            else if (document.inputform.Choose[1].checked){
                var dsPoint = mCom.PromptForPoint("Select the center of the t-shirt");
                //This is for the "Select on Screen" option
                //You must assign dsPoint.X, dsPoint.Y, and dsPoint.Z to the same variables
                //that are assigned in the "Enter Coordinates" option
                var X = dsPoint.X;
                var Y = dsPoint.Y;
                var Z = dsPoint.Z;
            }
            var size = document.getElementById("S").value;
            var W = 0;
            var H = 0;
            //Units are in centimeters
            if (size == "Small") {
                W = 45;
                H = 65;
            }
            else if (size == "Medium") {
                W = 55;
                H = 75;
            }
            else if (size == "Large") {
                W = 65;
                H = 85;
            }
            else if (size == "X-Large") {
                W = 75;
                H = 95;
            }
            else if (size == "XX-Large") {
                W = 85;
                H = 105;
            }
            var halfShirt = new Array();
            halfShirt[0] = X;
            halfShirt[1] = Y - H/2;
            halfShirt[2] = X + W/2;
            halfShirt[3] = Y - H/2;
            halfShirt[4] = X + W/2;
            halfShirt[5] = Y + H/4;
            halfShirt[6] = X + 3*W/4;
            halfShirt[7] = Y;
            halfShirt[8] = X + W;
            halfShirt[9] = Y + H/8;
            halfShirt[10] = X + W/2;
            halfShirt[11] = Y + H/2;
            halfShirt[12] = X + W/4;
            halfShirt[13] = Y + H/2;
            var shirtLine = mSkMgr.InsertPolyline2D(halfShirt, false);
            var collar = mSkMgr.InsertArc(X, Y + H/2, 0, W/4, 3/2*Math.PI, 2*Math.PI);
            var entityarray = new Array();
            var longarray = new Array();
            entityarray[0] = shirtLine;
            entityarray[1] = collar;
            longarray[0] = 27;
            longarray[1] = 23;
            mSkMgr.MirrorEntities(X, Y + H/2, 0, X, Y - H/2, 0, false, longarray, entityarray);
            printOut("Width: " + W + " Height: " + H);
            printOut("X: " + X + " Y: " + Y + " Z: " + Z);

            var lowerLeftArray = [0,0,0]
            var upperRightArray = [0,0,0]
            mApp.Zoom("dsZoomRange_Fit", lowerLeftArray, upperRightArray)
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>

    <title>Mirror Entities Example (JS)</title>

    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsCircle.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsBlockDefinition.js"></script>
    <script type="text/javascript" src="http://localhost:7776/APISDK/djLibrary/dsBlockInstance.js"></script>

    <!-- This is the main block where you place the JavaScript API code -->
    <script type="text/javascript">
        function printOut(str) {
            document.getElementById("OB").value += str + "\n";
            document.getElementById("OB").scrollTop += document.getElementById("OB").scrollHeight;
        }

        function runMacro() {
            //This is to catch any errors and exit out of the code
            //It displays an error in the output box
            try {
                apiCode();
            }
            catch (err) {
                printOut(djErrorString(err));
            }
        }
        function apiCode() {
            var mScript = new djScriptManager();
            var mApp = mScript.getApplication();

            //Abort any command currently running in DraftSight to avoid nested commands
            mApp.AbortRunningCommand();

            var mDoc = mApp.GetActiveDocument();
            var mModel = mDoc.GetModel();
            var mSkMgr = mModel.GetSketchManager();

    		var X = 0;
    		var Y = 0;
    		var Z = 0;
    		var R1 = 10;
			var R2 = 15;

    		var mCircle1 = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X, Y, Z, R1);
			var mCircle2 = mSkMgr.InsertCircle(X, Y, Z, R2);

			var longarray = new Array();
		    var entityarray = new Array();
			longarray[0] = 22;
		    longarray[1] = 22;
			entityarray[0] = mCircle1;
		    entityarray[1] = mCircle2;

			mDoc.CreateBlockDefinition("TestBlock", "Test Block", X, Y, Z, longarray, entityarray, "dsBlockDefinitionEntities_RemoveFromDrawing");

			var ScaleX = parseFloat(document.getElementById("X").value);
 		    var ScaleY = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Y").value);
		    var ScaleZ = parseFloat(document.getElementById("Z").value);

 		    <!-- var Rotation = parseFloat(document.getElementById("R").value); -->
 		    var Rotation = 0;

			var mCmdMsg = mApp.GetCommandMessage();
			var params = mCmdMsg.PromptForBlockInsertion("TestBlock", true, ScaleX, ScaleY, ScaleZ, false, Rotation);

			var PosX = params.X;
			var PosY = params.Y;
			var PosZ = params.Z;
			ScaleX = params.ScaleX;
			ScaleY = params.ScaleY;
			ScaleZ = params.ScaleZ;
			Rotation = params.Rotation;

			mSkMgr.InsertBlock2("TestBlock", PosX, PosY, PosZ, ScaleX, ScaleY, ScaleZ, Rotation);

            printOut("X scale factor: " + ScaleX + "\n");
            printOut("Y scale factor: " + ScaleY + "\n");
            printOut("Z scale factor: " + ScaleZ + "\n");

            //Type your JavaScript API code here
            mApp.releaseAll();
        }
    </script>
